# Natural Logistics Final Descriptions Compendium

This document consolidates the complete final English description and Chinese reference translation for all 100 problems. English is authoritative.

本文档汇总全部 100 道题的完整最终英文描述及中文参考译文，以英文版本为准。

## p01

Decision: `NO REWRITE REQUIRED` · [case notes](p01/rewrite_notes.md)

### Final English description

In this logistics scenario, we begin with several objects and locations. Package obj11, obj12, and obj13 are initially at location pos1. Similarly, package obj21, obj22, and obj23 start at location pos2. We have two trucks: truck tru1 is stationed at pos1, and truck tru2 is at pos2. Additionally, we have a single airplane, apn1, which is located at airport apt2. Our map comprises two cities: cit1 and cit2. City cit1 contains location pos1 and airport apt1, while city cit2 includes location pos2 and airport apt2. The ultimate objective is to relocate package obj11 and obj13 to airport apt1 and to move package obj21 and obj23 to location pos1.

### Chinese reference translation

在这个物流场景中，我们从若干对象和地点开始。包裹 obj11、obj12 和 obj13 最初位于地点 pos1。同样，包裹 obj21、obj22 和 obj23 起始于地点 pos2。我们有两辆卡车：卡车 tru1 停在 pos1，卡车 tru2 位于 pos2。此外，我们有一架飞机 apn1，它位于机场 apt2。我们的地图包含两座城市：cit1 和 cit2。城市 cit1 包含地点 pos1 和机场 apt1，而城市 cit2 包含地点 pos2 和机场 apt2。最终目标是将包裹 obj11 和 obj13 转移到机场 apt1，并将包裹 obj21 和 obj23 移至地点 pos1。

## p02

Decision: `NO REWRITE REQUIRED` · [case notes](p02/rewrite_notes.md)

### Final English description

In this logistics scenario, we begin with several packages, vehicles, and locations. The packages are: obj11, obj12, and obj13, all currently located at pos1, and obj21, obj22, and obj23, all currently located at pos2. There are two trucks: tru1 positioned at pos1, and tru2 positioned at pos2. We also have two cities: cit1 and cit2, each containing a set of locations. Within cit1, there are pos1 and an airport, apt1. Likewise, within cit2, there are pos2 and an airport, apt2. An airplane, apn1, is stationed at the airport apt2. Our objective is to relocate the packages such that obj12 remains at pos1 along with obj23, obj11 is transferred to apt1, obj22 is also moved to apt1, while obj13 and obj21 should both be at pos2.

### Chinese reference translation

在这个物流场景中，我们从若干包裹、车辆和地点开始。这些包裹是：obj11、obj12 和 obj13，它们目前都位于 pos1；以及 obj21、obj22 和 obj23，它们目前都位于 pos2。有两辆卡车：tru1 位于 pos1，tru2 位于 pos2。我们还有两座城市：cit1 和 cit2，每座城市都包含一组地点。cit1 内有 pos1 和一座机场 apt1。同样，cit2 内有 pos2 和一座机场 apt2。一架飞机 apn1 停在机场 apt2。我们的目标是重新安排这些包裹，使 obj12 留在 pos1、obj23 也位于 pos1，obj11 被运到 apt1、obj22 也被移到 apt1，而 obj13 和 obj21 都应位于 pos2。

## p03

Decision: `NO REWRITE REQUIRED` · [case notes](p03/rewrite_notes.md)

### Final English description

In the Logistics game, we start with the following initial conditions: There are nine packages, identified as obj11, obj12, obj13, obj21, obj22, obj23, obj31, obj32, and obj33. We have three trucks named tru1, tru2, and tru3. Additionally, there are three cities cited as cit1, cit2, and cit3, each with two locations, pos1 and apt1 in cit1, pos2 and apt2 in cit2, and pos3 and apt3 in cit3. Each city also has an airport, specifically apt1, apt2, and apt3. A single airplane, apn1, is currently stationed at airport apt1. Truck tru1, along with packages obj11, obj12, and obj13, is located at pos1. Meanwhile, truck tru2, and packages obj21, obj22, and obj23 are situated at pos2, and truck tru3, along with packages obj31, obj32, and obj33, are at pos3. The goal of the game is to ensure the following package deliveries: obj22 should remain at pos2, obj33 needs to be moved to apt1, obj12 is to be delivered to pos2, obj13 must reach apt3, obj31 should arrive at apt2, obj23 is to be transferred to apt1, and obj32 has to be relocated to pos1.

### Chinese reference translation

在这个物流游戏中，我们从以下初始条件开始：共有九个包裹，分别为 obj11、obj12、obj13、obj21、obj22、obj23、obj31、obj32 和 obj33。我们有三辆卡车，名为 tru1、tru2 和 tru3。此外，还有三座城市，分别为 cit1、cit2 和 cit3；每座城市有两个地点：cit1 中是 pos1 和 apt1，cit2 中是 pos2 和 apt2，cit3 中是 pos3 和 apt3。每座城市也各有一座机场，具体为 apt1、apt2 和 apt3。一架飞机 apn1 目前停在机场 apt1。卡车 tru1 连同包裹 obj11、obj12 和 obj13 位于 pos1。同时，卡车 tru2 和包裹 obj21、obj22、obj23 位于 pos2；卡车 tru3 连同包裹 obj31、obj32 和 obj33 位于 pos3。游戏目标是确保完成以下包裹交付：obj22 应留在 pos2，obj33 需要移至 apt1，obj12 要送到 pos2，obj13 必须到达 apt3，obj31 应到达 apt2，obj23 要转移到 apt1，而 obj32 必须重新安置到 pos1。

Similarly, obj21, obj22, and obj23 are at pos2 in city cit2, while obj31, obj32, and obj33 are located at pos3 in city cit3. Additionally, there are three airports: apt1 in city cit1, apt2 in city cit2, and apt3 in city cit3, with the airplane apn1 currently at apt3. ### Chinese reference translation

在这个物流游戏中，我们开始时在三个初始位置中的每一个都有三个包裹。具体来说，obj11、obj12 和 obj13 位于 pos1，而 pos1 在城市 cit1 中。同样，obj21、obj22 和 obj23 位于城市 cit2 的 pos2，而 obj31、obj32 和 obj33 位于城市 cit3 的 pos3。我们还有三辆卡车：tru1 停在 pos1，tru2 停在 pos2，tru3 停在 pos3。此外，有三座机场：城市 cit1 中的 apt1、城市 cit2 中的 apt2 和城市 cit3 中的 apt3；飞机 apn1 目前位于 apt3。目标是重新分配包裹，使 obj33 和 obj23 最终位于 apt1，obj11 到达 pos3，obj32 位于 pos1，obj13 被转移到 pos2，而 obj21 和 obj12 都被运到 apt3。

### Final English description

## p11

## p64

Decision: `REWRITE REQUIRED` · [case notes](p64/rewrite_notes.md)

### Final English description

In the Logistics game, I have a series of packages and transportation vehicles distributed across several cities and locations. Initially, for each integer i from 1 through 11, we have exactly three packages labeled by concatenating obj, the decimal representation of i, and one of 1, 2, or 3, for a total of 33 packages. There are also 11 trucks labeled from tru1 to tru11, each initially located at different positions, pos1 through pos11, across the cities cit1 to cit11. For each integer i from 1 through 11, the city labeled by concatenating cit and i contains the two locations labeled by concatenating pos and i and by concatenating apt and i, and the apt location is an airport; the three airplanes are apn1 at apt3, apn2 at apt2, and apn3 at apt11. 

 The initial setup is as follows: 
 - In city cit1, truck tru1 and packages obj11, obj12, and obj13 are initially at pos1. 
 - In city cit2, truck tru2 and packages obj21, obj22, and obj23 are initially at pos2. 
 - For each integer i from 3 through 11, the truck labeled by concatenating tru and i and the three packages labeled by concatenating obj, i, and one of 1, 2, or 3 are initially at the position labeled by concatenating pos and i. 

 My objective is to rearrange these packages to new specified destinations: 
 - I aim to have obj23 moved to pos4, obj92 to apt2, and obj101 to pos6. 
 - I would like obj21 to be at pos7, obj31 at apt7, and obj51 relocated to pos1. 
 - Further movements include obj113 to apt5, obj61 to pos7, and obj112 to pos2. 
 - Also obj62 should reach apt9, obj43 to apt4, and obj103 to pos6. 
 - As for obj11, it should be at apt6, while obj81 and obj93 should both be placed at apt11. 
 - Obj53 needs to go to apt3, obj32 to apt1, and obj102 should go to apt6. 
 - Obj41 must be at apt4, while obj83 should be at pos3 along with obj33. 
 - Other specific destinations include obj22 at pos7, obj12 at apt9, obj73 at pos2, and obj52 at apt8. 
 - Obj63 should arrive at apt3, obj91 at apt11, and obj82 should be at apt4. 
 - In addition, obj71 must reach pos1, obj13 should be positioned at pos8, and obj72 along with obj111 should respectively be moved to pos4 and apt9. 
 - Finally, obj42 needs to be transferred to pos2. 

 Organizing this logistical network requires careful planning to achieve the desired package arrangements across these different locations.

### Chinese reference translation

在物流游戏中，我有一系列分布在多座城市和地点的包裹及运输工具。初始时，对于从 1 到 11 的每个整数 i，恰好有三个包裹，其标签由 obj、i 的十进制表示以及 1、2、3 中的一个依次连接而成，共计 33 个包裹。另有 11 辆标签从 tru1 到 tru11 的卡车，它们初始时分别位于从 pos1 到 pos11 的不同位置，分布在从 cit1 到 cit11 的城市中。对于从 1 到 11 的每个整数 i，由 cit 与 i 连接形成标签的城市包含两个地点：由 pos 与 i 连接形成标签的地点，以及由 apt 与 i 连接形成标签的地点；其中 apt 地点是机场；三架飞机分别是位于 apt3 的 apn1、位于 apt2 的 apn2 和位于 apt11 的 apn3。

 初始设置如下：
 - 在城市 cit1，卡车 tru1 与包裹 obj11、obj12、obj13 初始时都在 pos1。
 - 在城市 cit2，卡车 tru2 与包裹 obj21、obj22、obj23 初始时都在 pos2。
 - 对于从 3 到 11 的每个整数 i，由 tru 与 i 连接形成标签的卡车，以及标签由 obj、i、1/2/3 中的一个依次连接而成的三个包裹，初始时都在由 pos 与 i 连接形成标签的位置。

 我的目标是将这些包裹重新安排到新的指定目的地：
 - 我希望 obj23 移到 pos4，obj92 移到 apt2，obj101 移到 pos6。
 - 我希望 obj21 在 pos7，obj31 在 apt7，obj51 移到 pos1。
 - 进一步的移动包括 obj113 到 apt5、obj61 到 pos7、obj112 到 pos2。
 - obj62 还应到达 apt9，obj43 到达 apt4，obj103 到达 pos6。
 - obj11 应在 apt6，而 obj81 和 obj93 都应在 apt11。
 - obj53 需要到 apt3，obj32 到 apt1，obj102 到 apt6。
 - obj41 必须在 apt4，而 obj83 应与 obj33 一同在 pos3。
 - 其他指定目的地包括 obj22 在 pos7、obj12 在 apt9、obj73 在 pos2、obj52 在 apt8。
 - obj63 应到达 apt3，obj91 到达 apt11，obj82 应在 apt4。
 - 此外，obj71 必须到达 pos1，obj13 应在 pos8，obj72 和 obj111 应分别移到 pos4 和 apt9。
 - 最后，obj42 需要转移到 pos2。

 要在这些不同地点之间实现期望的包裹安排，需要仔细规划这一物流网络。

The positions (pos1 through pos12) and airports (apt1 through apt12) are mapped to their corresponding cities. Notably, airports apt1 through apt12 additionally serve as airports within their respective cities. Three airplanes, apn1, apn2, and apn3, are located at apt5, apt2, and apt1, respectively. For every integer X from 1 through 12, truck truX and packages objX1, objX2, and objX3 are positioned at posX initially, where X is replaced by the same decimal numeral in every identifier (so X = 10 yields tru10, obj101, obj102, obj103, and pos10). Each positional location posX is in its respective city citX, and the airport aptX is also located in the same city citX.

Our goals for the Logistics game are to position the packages at targeted locations: Place obj82 at apt1, move obj103 to pos1, relocate obj101 to apt5, send obj31 to apt1, position obj11 at apt4, have obj43 at pos10, relocate obj42 to pos3, move obj12 to pos11, position obj112 at apt4, place obj83 at apt8, send obj22 to pos7, have obj53 remain at pos5, move obj41 to apt12, relocate obj123 to apt9, position obj93 at pos6, move obj32 to pos1, send obj113 to apt8, place obj23 at apt1, move obj13 to pos9, relocate obj52 to pos10, send obj33 to pos4, position obj63 at apt3, move obj121 to pos6, relocate obj91 to apt5, position obj51 at apt1, send obj21 to pos3, move obj81 to apt11, position obj111 at apt7, relocate obj62 to apt2, move obj122 to apt2, position obj92 at apt6, move obj71 to apt1, place obj73 at pos4, and lastly, relocate obj61 to pos10.

The desired goal is to have package obj11 remain at pos1 in cit1, to transport obj12 to apt1, to move obj13 and obj21 to pos2 in cit2, to get obj22 to apt2 in cit2, and to leave obj23 at pos2 in cit2.

Our cities, numbered cit1 to cit14, each contain a position and an airport; specifically, for every integer X from 1 through 14, posX is a location in citX and aptX is an airport location in citX. For every integer X from 1 through 14, truck truX and packages objX1, objX2, and objX3 are stationed at posX initially. Airplanes, on the other hand, start at specific airports: apn1 at apt3 in cit3, apn2 at apt4 in cit4, apn3 at apt8 in cit8, and apn4 at apt4 in cit4. Our goal is to strategically transport the packages listed below to their desired locations across the cities, employing the right combinations of trucks and airplanes. ### Final English description

The truck tru1 and packages obj11, obj12, and obj13 are located at pos1 in city cit1. Meanwhile, truck tru2 and packages obj21, obj22, and obj23 are at pos2 in city cit2. Our goal is to transport and arrange the packages as follows: obj23 should be at airport apt1 in city cit1, obj21 should remain at pos2 in city cit2, obj12 should be moved to airport apt2 in city cit2, obj13 needs to be at airport apt1 in city cit1, obj22 should also end up at airport apt1 in city cit1, and finally, obj11 is to be moved to pos2 in city cit2.
